      "text": "---\nstatus: draft\n---\n\n<a name=\"root\"></a>\n\n# INFRA-003: Fonbet hockey odds {#root}\n\n<a name=\"plain-language\"></a>\n\n## Plain language {#plain-language}\n\nFonbet hockey odds are stored with their exact settlement conditions and history.\n\n<a name=\"goal\"></a>\n\n## Goal {#goal}\n\nDo not mix the outcome in 60 minutes with the outcome with overtime and shootouts.\n\n<a name=\"governing-specs\"></a>\n\n## Governing specifications {#governing-specs}\n\nProduct boundaries: `specs/common/main.md`; mutual contracts and exact links are listed in #relationships. The document remains draft until the listed source/rules gates are closed.\n\n\n<a name=\"scope\"></a>\n\n## Scope {#scope}\n\nGet KHL odds from Fonbet. The current `src/providers/fonbet/odds.ts` uses football factor IDs and pairs of individual totals 0.5/1.5. The hockey adapter should not inherit their value by matching numeric ID. `src/machete/fixture-odds-sync.ts` and scheduler select football leagues and rounds: their behavior is preserved.\n\nThe transport available in the code uses `events/listBase?lang=ru&scopeMarket=1600`. The specific completeness of hockey markets, factor IDs, and stability of unloading have not been tested by this task. ODD-00 - mandatory probe on the current payload before implementing the parser. Base URL configurable; new domains are not guessed. This is analytical data: placing bets is not included.\n\n<a name=\"markets\"></a>\n\n## Market semantics {#markets}\n\nThe implemented circuit WI-023 is limited to prematch 1/X/2 for 60 minutes. Probe 2026-09-14: 12 of real KHL events from the hockey tree (root 2, regular season 13283), Fonbet catalog `line/factorsCatalog/tables`, “Outcomes” table: 921=1, 922=X, 923=2. The “Final Victory” table is separate and not used. The conditions of regular time are confirmed by the Fonbet hockey rule 10.1. The dictionary is rechecked daily for names and positions; other markets remain untested. A compressed set of real examples is stored in src/providers/fonbet/fixtures/khl-line-20260914.json. The “Hosts/Guests” aggregate, child periods and live are excluded. eventBlocks.state=blocked immediately pauses the trio.\n\nRequired market key: `providerEventId + marketType + settlementScope + period + selection + line + marketVersion`. Values ​​of `settlementScope`: `REGULATION_60`, `INCLUDING_OT_SO`, `INCLUDING_OT_NO_SO`, `UNKNOWN`; period `FULL_GAME|P1|P2|P3|OT`. UNKNOWN is saved for diagnostic purposes and is not used in prognosis.\n\n| Market | Acceptable interpretation |\n|---|---|\n| 1/X/2 for 60 minutes | Home win/draw/guest win in regular time; need a complete triplet of one photo |\n| Winner with OT and shootouts | Two outcomes of the match; does not replace the probability of winning in regular time |\n| General total / individual total | Save the number line, side, period and real calculation conditions; total 5.5 for 60 minutes and 5.5 with OT/shootouts - different markets |\n| Handicap | Signed line from the point of view of the selected command, push conditions; do not output ready-made xG directly |\n| Clean sheet / correct score / other | Only with a confirmed market dictionary. No line - null, no fictitious quotes |\n\nThe procedure for calculating a winning shootout goal in totals is part of the provider rules; it cannot automatically be considered a player's goal/goalkeeper's goal. The Odds model should take into account the probability of OT and model the distribution of team outcomes separately from G/A/GA and official FP.\n\nFrom the complete mutually exclusive outcomes of one market/snapshot, approximate probabilities can be obtained by removing the proportional margin: `p_i = (1/odds_i) / sum(1/odds_j)`. This is a model, not a bookmaker fact. Incomplete pair/triple, odds ≤1, NaN, wrong side or incompatible conditions → probability=null. Integer/Asian totals with return cannot be interpreted as a simple binary pair without a push model.\n\nCannot `P(ОТ-победы) = P(победы с ОТ) − P(победы за 60)` without a consistent joint model: independently refined margins may produce inconsistent probabilities. Fit four outcomes W60/W_OTSO/L_OTSO/L60 with the sum 1, non-negativity and fit quality check. For a bad fit, use a proven hockey baseline with a warning, do not trim negative values, and declare the result accurate.\n\n<a name=\"matching\"></a>\n\n## Event mapping {#matching}\n\n1. Select hockey and a confirmed KHL competition for the current season from the sport tree; exclude NHL/MHL/friendlies, simulations and live from prematch calculations. The name \"hockey\" by itself is not enough.\n2. Bring clubs through verified alias → internal KhlTeam IDs. Home/away save; do not guess the reverse orientation.\n3. UTC start time with initial tolerance ±15 minutes, then exactly one candidate with both teams, competition and date. To transfer, use existing external event mapping and recheck, do not expand the window to several days.\n4. Parent event of the match is separated from child events of periods/markets. Same names in multiple events do not create multiple matches.\n5. Zero/multiple candidates → UNMATCHED/AMBIGUOUS, separate trial queue. Save source, reasons and matching version; do not match on the same team.\n\n<a name=\"snapshots\"></a>\n\n## Pictures and freshness {#snapshots}\n\nStore immutable revisions of the normalized set of markets (INFRA-002), fetchedAt, sourcePublishedAt if available, completeFeed flag, payloadHash, parserVersion, status. Matching the last hash only updates lastSeenAt; a new price, line or status creates a revision. Returning A→B→A creates a third revision, even if the raw bytes are already in the cache. The time series must distinguish between the moment of observation and the moment of change, and the forecast refers to an accurate revision.\n\nStatuses: `AVAILABLE`, `SUSPENDED`, `WITHDRAWN`, `STALE`, `UNMATCHED`. In case of obvious stopping/removal, apply immediately. Absence on one request means WITHDRAWN only if a complete successful snapshot of the specified scope is proven; for delta-feed - only explicit tombstone. Timeout/error/truncated page means stale, not mass withdrawal. Old values ​​are visible with the date, but do not become the current line after disappearance.\n\nInitial polling: once every 15 minutes for the next 7 days, once every 60 s for matches in the next 6 hours, with allowed limits. Freshness for recommendations: ≤5 min within 6 hours before the match, ≤30 min further; after prematch starts, the line is unusable regardless of TTL. The distant week may be without coefficients, the UI shows the absence and the source of the baseline. No-vig calculations only within one snapshot, no “over yesterday + under now” pairs.\n\nRetention: raw answers are included in the general KHL raw budget from INFRA-001; compact line changes - season +90 days with permitted storage. Indexes match/market/observedAt; historical reading is limited by spacing and pagination. Statuses and disappeared markets are involved in hash/invalidation.\n\n<a name=\"acceptance\"></a>\n\n## Acceptance criteria {#acceptance}\n\n- ODD-00: current Fonbet samples of at least 10 KHL events, all necessary types of markets and a factor/period/settlement dictionary with proven human-readable names; HTTP and stability tested with staging. If the required market does not exist, the forecast is unavailable and limited.\n- ODD-01: separate 1X2 for 60 and winner with OT/shootout are saved and displayed separately; identical line in different scopes does not merge.\n- ODD-02: ambiguous clubs, two events on the same day, team reshuffle, transfer and child period do not lead to false mapping.\n- ODD-03: the complete triple gives the sum of the probabilities 1 with the tolerance 1e-6; an incomplete/invalid line gives null; removed and stale lines are excluded from the current calculation.\n- ODD-04: replay payload does not create a new revision; explicit withdrawal and outage are different; data as of the backtest date does not contain later quotes.\n- ODD-05: existing football odds tests and scheduler tests pass without changing expectations; hockey syncs do not write FixtureOddsSnapshot.\n\n<a name=\"relationships\"></a>\n\n## Related specifications {#relationships}\n\n`spec://modules/khl/INFRA-001-khl-data-ingestion#operations`, `spec://modules/khl/INFRA-002-khl-storage-and-api#schema`, `spec://modules/khl/FEAT-003-khl-projections-and-optimizer#forecast`.\n\n<a name=\"changelog\"></a>\n\n## Changelog {#changelog}\n\n- 2026-09-28: English documentation, repaired document references, and GitHub navigation anchors (WI-039).\n\n- 2026-09-14: checked 12 current KHL events and dictionary 1/X/2 for 60 minutes; separate parser/sync, strict aliases, freshness and beta correction of the opponent (WI-023).       "text": "---\nstatus: active\n---\n\n<a name=\"root\"></a>\n\n# FEAT-001: Automatic global ranking strategy {#root}\n\n<a name=\"plain-language\"></a>\n\n## Plain language {#plain-language}\n\nThe squad planner gains a global-ranking mode, labeled `По глобальному рейтингу` in the UI. Rank, points, the gap to the leader, and field size are retrieved from Sports.ru or FPL. Squad selection departs from the popular template only within the permitted EP loss.\n\nThe full text dated 2026-09-06 is preserved below and in `docs/archive/planning/GLOBAL_STRATEGY_SPEC.md`. The user authorized this contract; implementation readiness and verification evidence are tracked separately in WI-001.\n\n<a name=\"goal\"></a>\n\n## 1. Goal {#goal}\n\nGive the user with the linked command a reproducible global K factor and valid squad with explicit EP comparison. If there are problems with the source, the usual selection remains available.\n\n<a name=\"governing-specs\"></a>\n\n## 2. Governing specifications {#governing-specs}\n\n- Related: `spec://modules/machete/FEAT-002-global-strategy-formula#root`\n\n<a name=\"scope\"></a>\n\n## 3. Scope {#scope}\n\n<a name=\"scope.in\"></a>\n\n### 3.1. In scope {#scope.in}\n\nTwo providers, automatic global context, pure K and strategyScore functions, application in squad selection, starting lineup, captain and transfers, EP loss budget, explanation and saving of settings.\n\n<a name=\"scope.out\"></a>\n\n### 3.2. Out of scope {#scope.out}\n\nSimulation of opponents, probability of victory, mini-leagues, new EP engine, automatic sending of transfers, full-fledged effective ownership model.\n\n<a name=\"actors\"></a>\n\n## 4. Participants and triggers {#actors}\n\nFantasy-user with a linked Sports.ru profile or FPL entry. The admin does not set rank manually. The client cannot be sent someone else’s rank or entry as a personal setting.\n\n<a name=\"scenarios\"></a>\n\n## 5. Scenarios {#scenarios}\n\nThe user enables the mode instead of balanced/reliable/upside. The UI shows the location, field size, backlog, remaining rounds, K, base and suggested predictions and reason. When K=0 - “Neutral strategy”. If the data is unsuitable, use the usual selection.\n\n<a name=\"data\"></a>\n\n## 6. Data and state {#data}\n\nSports price synchronization, provider snapshots and data-quality audits share the active source registry. Active scope follows the current season and explicit tournament calendar, not metadata updatedAt or stale environment scope lists. Retained finished tournaments remain visible as archives with integrity/count diagnostics and no ongoing freshness SLA. Only existing explicitly reviewed identity exclusions affect the eligible price-mapping denominator; unresolved players remain failures under unchanged thresholds. The home page uses active/user-selected leagues and separate successful price, statistics, lineup-change and forecast-calculation timestamps; missing/stale facts remain visible.\n\nContext: N, rank, team and leader points, T/R, ER for no more than 6 completed rounds, ownership of the same provider/tournament/season. GlobalContestStrategyState, UserGlobalStrategyState and GlobalStrategyRecommendation models. The setting is GLOBAL_AUTO in existing JSON filters.\n\n<a name=\"contracts\"></a>\n\n## 7. Contracts {#contracts}\n\nThe desktop pool remains virtualized and touch pool remains paginated at 18 players. Column selection and its existing persisted preferences are also available on touch screens; expanded mobile analytics use the selected metrics, including FFO and rotation risk, with touch/keyboard explanations. Confidence percentages have a visible data-quality label. Resize separators are focusable and support arrows, Shift acceleration, Home/End bounds and Enter reset, with current/min/max ARIA values; pointer resize and double-click reset remain. Pool rendering, forecast explanations, projection arithmetic and team-strength inputs have separate responsibility modules while preserving planner public exports and recommendations.\n\nProduction full Squad pool refreshes run in a terminating child process so native database working memory is released after each cycle. The scheduler keeps full and incremental work serialized until that child exits; parent shutdown terminates its child. Refresh hours, bootstrap-only-missing behavior, atomic READY publication, retained revisions, complete player data and request-time loading remain unchanged. Development without the production bundle keeps the same in-process implementation.\n\nPure functions in `src/machete/global-strategy.ts` and `src/machete/global-strategy-config.ts`. Loading the context into server/global-strategy-context.ts. `GET /api/machete/squads/global-strategy`. Integration into `src/machete/squad_logic.ts`, worker and `FantasySquadPlanner.tsx`.\n\n`PATCH` of this route binds only the team of the associated profile in the selected season. `POST` maintains a compact recommendation audit: the server checks its own context and budget, and the sent forecast marks `CLIENT_EP_REPORTED`; This is not server-side verification of the forecast. The start and captain are selected separately for each round, the bench gives 0 EP until the auto-replacement model appears. Old pool snapshots are read by the regular UI and are sequentially rebuilt by workers in the absence of `providerIdentityVersion: 1`; incomplete mapping disables GLOBAL_AUTO until rebuilding.\n\n<a name=\"player-identity\"></a>\n\n### Player identity and availability {#player-identity}\n\nProvider/core player-name comparison folds non-decomposing Latin letters using\ntheir CLDR Latin-ASCII equivalents (including ø/o, ł/l, æ/ae and ß/ss), before\nthe existing accent and punctuation normalization. Display names and source\nidentities are preserved. Name folding never relaxes uniqueness, birth-date,\nclub or existing manual mapping conflict checks.\n\nThe existing player-name search checks both the provider display name and\ncanonical FotMob name, including public nicknames such as Oso. Accent/Latin\nletter folding applies to search while preserving Cyrillic letters and the\ndisplay names. The search field, filters and export selection retain their flow.\n\nShared canonical player imports preserve an established birthday when a roster\nor match payload does not contain one. A supplied non-null birthday remains an\nexplicit observation; a newly created player may still have an unknown birthday.\n\nAn unmapped Sports price observed within 48 hours may resolve against the\ncanonical FotMob catalog when its current club resolves uniquely, its source\nstat identity supplies a canonical Latin name with at least two tokens, and\nboth the complete birthday and a strong name comparison agree. Existing\nuniqueness and competing-price checks still apply. Missing or contradictory\nbirthdays and stale observations cannot authorize this fallback. Availability\ncomes from the current provider price; no shared roster is activated and no\nstatistics or starter status are invented. Reviewed European club aliases are\nexplicit pairs, never a fuzzy cross-club match.\n\nAn explicitly reviewed `EXCLUDED` price identity remains excluded during\nautomatic mapping and normal price refresh, regardless of the recorded\nexclusion reason. An exact catalog or roster candidate cannot undo that\ndecision; returning the card to mapping requires an explicit admin action.\n\nFPL automatic identity matching still requires one exact normalized full name\ninside the mapped current club. Compare FotMob position codes using the existing\nfantasy position groups; LW/RW may be FPL MID, while a goalkeeper/outfield\ncontradiction and unknown roles remain rejected. Successful mapping changes\ninvalidate the price import's format-based idempotency key.\n\nEvery fresh FPL price with an agreeing MATCHED provider player map, canonical\nplayer and club may supply a virtual current roster row to the planner, including\nwhen the shared FotMob membership is inactive or absent. Price foreign keys\nalone cannot authorize an override. Apply provider positions and current clubs\nfor that pool without activating shared memberships or replacing their starter\nflags. Existing provider rules, saved selections and source-limited forecasts\nretain their behavior.\n\n<a name=\"transfer-rules\"></a>\n\n### Provider transfer rules {#transfer-rules}\n\nEvery squad-planning strategy uses three Sports.ru transfers per round, except\nChampionship 2026/2027 provider tour `2391` (11th tour, deadline\n2026-10-16T19:00:00Z) and Champions League 2026/2027 provider tour `2755`\n(third tour, deadline 2026-10-20T16:45:00Z), which each allow six. Match these\nexceptions by provider tour identity and deadline, independently of the local ordinal. Other tours retain\nthree; unused exceptional transfers do not carry forward. Round counters,\nsuggestions, save validation, saved-plan rollover and legacy opening allowances\nuse the allowance of the corresponding tour. Reading an older Sports.ru pool\nsnapshot applies the current transfer rule immediately without rebuilding its\nplayer data or mutating its stored metadata. Russian and English descriptions\nstate the active tour allowance without claiming accumulation.\n\nFPL retains its separate provider rules: one new free transfer per round,\nunused free transfers bank up to five, and extra transfers cost four points.\n\n<a name=\"club-limits\"></a>\n\n### Provider club limits {#club-limits}\n\nThe user's corrected requirement is authoritative: club limits and transfer\nquotas are separate rules.\n\nSports.ru Championship (48), Netherlands (57), Portugal (61) and Turkey (71)\nallow two players from one club; the third is rejected by manual selection,\nvalidation and automatic selection. Import defaults use the explicit league-ID\nmatrix. Correct the erroneously raised default-three contests only for Sports.ru 2026/2027 in\nthese four leagues, preserving other competitions and historical seasons.\nThe Squad snapshot shell reads the current contest's club limit without\nrewriting cached player payloads or saved squads; stale snapshot metadata cannot\nrestore the former limit. Explicit operator contest limits and FPL rules retain\ntheir existing behavior.\n\n<a name=\"errors\"></a>\n\n## 8. Errors and validation {#errors}\n\nINVALID_INPUT, INSUFFICIENT_SCORE_HISTORY, INCOMPLETE_CALENDAR, UNAVAILABLE, NEUTRAL, FINISHED. Do not substitute T=38, ER=70 or ownership=0 if data is missing.\n\n<a name=\"traceability\"></a>\n\n## 9. Status: ready for implementation; The function code and migrations have not yet been created.\n\nRelated rationale and formulas: [GLOBAL_STRATEGY_FORMULA_RECOMMENDATIONS.md](../../../docs/archive/planning/GLOBAL_STRATEGY_FORMULA_RECOMMENDATIONS.md).\n\n## 1. Result for the user\n\nThe “By Global Rating” mode appears in the scheduler. The user links the Sports.ru profile or FPL entry, selects an existing tournament/season and turns on the mode. Field size, place, points, gap, possession and remaining rounds are loaded automatically. Manual entry of these indicators and mini-leagues are not needed.\n\nWhen selecting, the following are shown:\n\n- global place and field size;\n- team points and gap from the leader;\n- remaining available rounds;\n- strategy intensity K/Kmax, separate from probabilities;\n- base and proposed forecasts, expected loss, allowed limit;\n- date of data and brief reason for recommendation.\n\nExample: “250 from 500 · backlog of 100 · remaining 3 of the round. squad forecast: 69.4; basic version: 70.0; inferior to 0.6 points. A less popular squad was chosen within the acceptable loss.”\n\nWhen K=0 display “Neutral strategy”. Do not promise protection of first place or probability of victory. The mode is chosen instead of balanced/reliable/upside, rather than being added on top of their heuristics. Existing modes retain their behavior when the function is disabled.\n\n## 2. Limits of the first version\n\nIncluded: two providers; automatic global context; individual configurations; pure functions K and strategyScore; uniform application in the selection of the squad, starting lineup, captain and transfers; limiting EP loss; explanation; saving settings; data quality check.\n\nNot included: simulation of all opponents, probability of final victory, mini-leagues, new EP engine, automatic sending of transfers to the provider’s website, independent change of available bonuses, full-fledged effective ownership model. The current squad export/save transport is not extended by this feature.\n\nThe main mathematical mode is a limited deviation from the global pattern, taking into account the lag from the leader. The title and analytics should not replace it with maximizing the expected rank.\n\n## 3. Data sources\n\n### Sports.ru\n\nUse existing client `src/lib/providers/sports-ru-fantasy.ts`, extending DTO and read queries:\n\n1. User profile → `fantasyQueries.squads(input: {userID, isActiveTournament:true})`. Select a squad strictly according to the linked providerSquadId and seasonID. If there are several suitable commands without an explicit link, ask to select a command, do not choose arbitrarily.\n2. `squads(input:{squadID,seasonID}) → seasonScoreInfo {place score totalPlaces}` - personal position. The request has been verified by a live response.\n3. `tournament(...).currentSeason {id totalSquadsCount tours {...}}` - season and calendar.\n4. `rating.squads(input:{entityID:seasonID,entityType:SEASON,sortOrder:DESC,pageSize:2,pageNum:1})` → `scoreInfo {place score totalPlaces}` - leader and second. Don't pass leagueID. Save timestamp; do not save the names of opponents if they are not needed.\n5. Ownership - existing `seasonPlayer.status.selectedBy`, binding by providerPlayerId. Use the same tournament and season, not Foontasy as the primary source of strategy.\n6. Official points of completed rounds - `squadTourInfo(input:{squadID,tourID}).scoreInfo.score`. The presence of the field is confirmed by the schema; Before implementing ER, check the live values ​​for completed rounds and their semantics. Do not take `seasonScoreInfo.averageScore=0` as a reliable average.\n\n`totalSquadsCount` and `totalPlaces` may have different semantics for including commands before the first result. N must correspond to the rating: totalPlaces from the agreed rating slice takes precedence. The second number is diagnostic; do not replace N with it without checking.\n\n### FPL\n\n1. `/api/bootstrap-static/`: total_players, items with selected_by_percent, events and average_entry_score.\n2. `/api/entry/{entryId}/`: summary_overall_rank, summary_overall_points, current_event; The global league is determined by the Overall system record. ID 314 is confirmed as of the research date, do not consider it a universal eternal constant.\n3. `/api/leagues-classic/{overallLeagueId}/standings/`: first page, rank=1 and total leader, last_updated_data. Do not download the entire multimillion-dollar rating.\n4. `/api/entry/{entryId}/history/`: completed team rounds for score scale. Expand client and exact allowlist `scripts/fpl-vpn-relay.mjs` for standings/history. Do not weaken the verification of origin and arbitrary paths. Standings confirmed by web request; the server path through relay is checked during implementation after adding the allowed route.\n5. Fix create and update prices in `src/server/fpl-price-sync.ts` by writing selectedByPercent. Update sync format version if snapshot/hash deduplication will otherwise skip recovery. Existing data can be restored by normal successful synchronization; check 653/653 or the current pool size, do not include 653 in the code.\n\n### General rules\n\nWe need explicit provider, contestId, season, entry/squad ID, source and time of each component. Only finite percentages are allowed in [0,100]; 0 is acceptable. FPL ownership cannot be taken from Sports.ru EPL. Normal ownership and captain exposure are different metrics.\n\nLeader, team points and rank refer to the same stage of recalculation. Official completed round preferred. During the recalculation, do not mix the old rank with live points. If consistency cannot be confirmed, K is not calculated; standard selection remains available. For each adapter, define a contract test for temporal consistency on the stored anonymized response.\n\n## 4. ER and calendar\n\nER - the average of official points of the linked team for the last no more than 6 completed rounds of participation. Use the same policy for two providers; store sampleCount and usedRoundIds. The round in which the team scored 0 is an observation, not a pass. Do not throw out negative values ​​for the sake of a positive average. No observations or average ≤0 - `INSUFFICIENT_SCORE_HISTORY`; do not substitute 70 silently. For 1–2 observations, show a small sample, but allow calculation; the early-season u itself severely limits K. This estimate is a scale, not a prediction of future points, nor is it the average variance of the field.\n\nFPL average_entry_score to store for later verification/calibration; do not change the ER source unnoticed mid-season. Own played bonuses can affect ER; in the first version this is a reflected limitation. Later, the source of the scale or the exclusion of bonus rounds is changed only by the new version of the profile.\n\nT — the total number of rounds of the current global standings, including those not yet played; R is the number of rounds of this competition with a still available solution deadline. Exactly at deadlineAt the round is no longer available. FPL double round - one gameweek, not two matches. An empty round exists in the calendar and does not disappear due to the lack of matches for the selected player. If the deadline has passed, the current unfinished round is not included in R.\n\nFor future planning, all subsequent R are considered relative to the corresponding deadline, but the current K in the first version is fixed at the time of the request: do not invent a future rank/G. Show that this is a strategy for the current situation.\n\nSports.ru cannot consider the last known 8 rounds of the Champions League a full tournament until it is confirmed that the standings are limited to them. The calendar policy is set by tournament and phase. For unknown T - `INCOMPLETE_CALENDAR`; this tournament does not receive an automatic K until a valid policy is added. No default T=38 for everyone.\n\n## 5. Compute Core Contract\n\nSuggested new files: `src/machete/global-strategy.ts`, `src/machete/global-strategy-config.ts`. Pure functions without Prisma, fetch, global cache, Date.now and modification of input objects.\n\n```ts\ntype Provider = \"SPORTS_RU\" | \"FPL\";\n\ntype GlobalStrategyConfig = {\n  version: string;\n  provider: Provider;\n  tournamentKey: string;\n  kMax: number;               // старт 0.8, (0, 1]\n  rankWeight: number;         // 0.4\n  gapWeight: number;          // 0.6; сумма весов = 1\n  gapScaleRounds: number;    // 3, >0\n  urgencyExponent: number;   // 2, >0\n  ownershipBonusScale: number; // 0.15, >=0\n  maxExpectedPointsLossFraction: number; // 0.03, [0,1)\n};\n\ntype GlobalStrategyContext = {\n  provider: Provider;\n  contestId: string;\n  season: string;\n  providerSquadId: string;\n  revision: string;\n  fieldSize: number;\n  rank: number;\n  managerPoints: number;\n  leaderPoints: number;\n  totalRounds: number;\n  remainingRounds: number;\n  roundScoreScale: number;\n  scoreSampleCount: number;\n  standingsRoundId: string;\n  observedAt: string;\n  expiresAt: string;\n};\n\ntype GlobalStrategyEvaluation = {\n  status: \"READY\" | \"NEUTRAL\" | \"UNAVAILABLE\" | \"FINISHED\";\n  k: number | null;\n  configVersion: string;\n  contextRevision: string | null;\n  rankRisk: number | null;\n  gapRisk: number | null;\n  urgency: number | null;\n  maxLossFraction: number;\n  reasonCodes: string[];\n};\n\nevaluateGlobalStrategy(context, config, now): GlobalStrategyEvaluation;\nscoreGlobalStrategyPlayer({ expectedPoints, ownershipPercent }, evaluation, config);\nevaluateGlobalStrategyPlan({ baseline, candidate }, evaluation, config);\n```\n\nThe signatures of the last three functions should be specified by types during implementation; required semantics:\n\n- `now` is transmitted externally; freshness is checked deterministically.\n- INVALID_INPUT: fractional/unsafe rank/N/R/T, rank out [1,N], R out [0,T], NaN/Infinity, non-positive ER, non-matching profile/provider/tournament, negative G, rank=1 at positive G. Unusable inputs cannot be “corrected” with an arbitrary clamp.\n- N≤1 with agreed rank and points - NEUTRAL; R=0 - FINISHED; leader/G=0 - NEUTRAL. For a correct one-day T=1,R=1 without played rounds - NEUTRAL, since there is no season position for adaptation yet.\n- READY: formulas q/g/u/K from recommendations. The configuration is validated once, but the kernel does not trust invalid numbers.\n- UNAVAILABLE returns k=null and a specific reason; it is not a numerical zero risk. The default selection runs the existing balanced.\n- scoreGlobalStrategyPlayer returns `{expectedPoints, strategyBonus, strategyScore}`; the original expectedPoints is not changed.\n- evaluateGlobalStrategyPlan returns `{eligible, expectedPointsLoss, maxExpectedPointsLoss, strategyScoreDelta, reasonCodes}`.\n- Calculations do not round before display; budget comparison error ≤1e-6 points, do not use rounded UI numbers.\n\n## 6. Optimization and transfers\n\n1. Fix source EP and horizon H: use one selected formula/consensus in both passes. For FFO H=1 if there are no subsequent forecasts. K does not change the forecast engine.\n2. Get a basic solution for the total expected points with the same budget, positions, team limits, fixations, exceptions, rules and available transfers. Remove old ownership/upside/form bonuses from this new basic pass; Leave some old regimes as they are.\n3. Get strategic candidates by strategyScore. The bonus is considered at the player-round level and receives the same start/captainship multiplier as the corresponding EP. The bench accounting is the same as the base estimator and is documented; do not count all 15 players as starting ones. If there is no autocorrect model, do not promise the exact bench EP.\n4. Evaluate the real EPs of each final decision again after selecting the XI/captain. Check budget B from recommendations. The captain of the next round uses the EP of the next round, not the amount for H rounds. For multi-round plans, captaincy and start are taken into account for each round; if no future plan is specified, apply the same explicit policy in both passes.\n5. The basic solution is to always include the final candidates in the set. Select the allowed maximum strategyScore; in case of equality - more EP, less transfer penalty, fewer moves, stable ID order. The strategic delta must be strictly positive beyond the error, otherwise the basic solution.\n6. For transfers B is considered relative to the best available plan, including “change nothing”, based on the full EP of the roster after penalties. Count the difference between incoming and outgoing bonuses, and not the bonus of only purchased ones. This removes the motivation for meaninglessly trading two equally rare players.\n7. Non-positive netHorizonDelta is only allowed in the new mode if the plan passes B and improves the strategic assessment. Change early eliminations near the lines 1987 and 2016 `squad_logic.ts`; in old modes they are preserved. The Lost Points warning must remain visible.\n8. You cannot add the original ownershipEdgeValue and transferCandidateScore bonus on top of the new bonus. The new mode uses one source of strategy and a single context for the captain and XI as well.\n9. Early selection of eight candidates for the position now may lose the necessary options. Form a limited pool of candidates based on EP and strategyScore, including the basic solution and fixed players. Do not increase the search space without a limit; for v1, limit the list to 16 per position and maintain the overall limit of 120 000 transfer combination evaluations. When the limit is reached, specify searchTruncated=true.\n10. The current search is heuristic. Base EP is the best found in a limited search, not a proven global maximum. Among the final candidate union, redefine the base as the maximum of the true EP and retest B; otherwise, the stronger basic option found by the strategic passage will remain unaccounted for. Any termination of the search returns the best valid option, including the base one.\n\nA common possession for a captain is an approximation of a global template. Don't call it effective ownership. The rarity bonus does not override user-set minute/availability restrictions; Do not add new arbitrary medical or start filters to this mode.\n\n## 7. Integration into an existing project\n\n| File/layer | Change |\n|---|---|\n| `src/lib/providers/sports-ru-fantasy.ts` | DTO and limited global ranking/point requests |\n| `src/lib/providers/fpl.ts` | DTO profile/history/standings, analysis of total_players and completed rounds |\n| `src/server/fpl-price-sync.ts` | Saving selectedByPercent, restoring via sync |\n| `scripts/fpl-vpn-relay.mjs` | Exact allowed paths standings/history |\n| new server/global-strategy-context.ts | Loading, reconciliation, freshness and context normalization |\n| `src/machete/squad_logic.ts` | K and evaluation of plans at all listed selection points |\n| `fantasy-squad-worker-contract.ts` and handler | Passing compact context and version, single result |\n| `FantasySquadPlanner.tsx` | New mode, position data, explanation and comparison EP |\n| API squads and saving filters | Explicit allowed mode setting, reading/saving without losing other filters |\n\nSuggested read endpoint: `GET /api/machete/squads/global-strategy?squadId=...`. Authorization is the same as for squads; checking the owner of the local squad; provider/contest/entry is determined by the saved binding. Do not trust the rank, points or arbitrary entry sent by the client when requesting personal settings. The response contains only normalized data, status/reasons and configVersion, not the raw profile with name/contacts.\n\nInclude contextRevision, configVersion, poolRevision, forecastSource and H in the Worker state. When changing squad/provider/pool or a new request, ignore the old response; override obsolete work with existing machinery. Recalculating K does not by itself trigger a reload of the entire player pool.\n\n## 8. Storage, cache, duplicates and memory\n\nSeparate the general and personal parts:\n\n- GlobalContestStrategyState: one current entry per provider/contestId/season, N fields, leader points, rating stage, T/R/calendarRevision, timestamps and sourceRevision. Local freshness logic does not change the provider's calendar.\n- UserGlobalStrategyState: one current entry per userId/contestId/providerSquadId/season, rank/points, history of no more than 6 values ​​for ER, link to the agreed general revision and timestamps. The new binding does not reuse the previous context.\n- User setting: GLOBAL_AUTO mode in existing JSON filters; not retain K as the primary source of truth. The update merges keys and does not erase roundPlans and custom formulas.\n\nThese are proposed new Prisma models, not existing tables. Add unique keys and migration. Do not duplicate price, ownership and player pool in these entries. Reloading one slice - upsert; updates to the general/personal parts are published only after consistency has been checked. If the source does not provide a general revision, use its available timestamps/round IDs and sequential reading check; the absence of an agreed cut is clearly indicated.\n\nIn-memory cache via ExpiringPromiseCache:\n\n- general context: up to 32 records / 1 MiB, TTL 15 minutes outside of live recalculation, 5 minutes when updating the round;\n- personal: up to 256 records / 2 MiB, TTL 5 minutes;\n- total limit of concurrent new rating requests - 2 per provider; combine identical keys into one promise;\n- retry: no more than one per temporary network error, with delay and without an infinite loop; 429 process by Retry-After and show the available state;\n- old data can be shown with a date, but after expiresAt a new strategic result cannot be built. Deadline rollover invalidates the corresponding key regardless of TTL;\n- ownershipRevision, forecastRevision, contextRevision and configVersion are included in the selection result key. Personal K results are not added to the player’s general cache;\n- existing shared pool restrictions 96 MiB and overlay 32 MiB, as well as retention 3 revisions are preserved.\n\nFor future verification, save a compact snapshot of the actually implemented recommendation with source revisions, player IDs/multipliers, baseline/candidate EP, K and loss budget. Deduplication by user/squad/decisionRound/inputHash/configVersion; store the last 60 days. Export complete historical data for the study separately by explicit task; Do not accumulate daily copies of all players for each user.\n\nWhen implementing, measure memory before/after repeated requests and check stabilization after eviction/TTL. One RSS snapshot cannot confirm the absence of a leak. Do not clear operational user data for this feature.\n\n## 9. Tests and acceptance criteria\n\nMathematics:\n\n- Check all five numerical examples from the recommendations with tolerance 1e-6.\n- All other things being equal, deterioration of rank, increase in G, decrease in R do not reduce K; 0≤K≤Kmax for N=500 and N=10 506 303.\n- Leader/tied on points, N=1, R=0, start of season, T=1, tied rank, wrong numbers, negative G, no ER.\n- O=0/100 are valid; null and out-of-range values ​​do not give a premium and block GLOBAL_AUTO for a particular set of candidates. The usual EP selection is available.\n- EP=0/negative does not receive a positive bonus. Formulas do not mutate inputs; repeated calls give the same result.\n\nIntegration and optimization:\n\n- The same football player in FPL and Sports.ru receives its own provider context, EP and ownership. The provider mismatch is not corrected by fallback to another.\n- FPL selectedByPercent is saved in both create and update; repeated sync does not create duplicates, and restoration is not skipped due to an old hash.\n- Transfer penalty, no-op, identical players for several moves, team/positional limits, captain and bench do not break B’s budget.\n- Regression example: a rare player with a noticeably lower EP does not displace a strong one if the solution exceeds B.\n- A plan with a small negative EP delta can only appear in GLOBAL_AUTO if the strategic delta is positive and B is met. The loss is visible in the UI.\n- The search does not lose the base squad due to the cutting off of candidates. If all strategic options are rejected, the base is returned; lack of a valid base returns an error from the normal optimizer.\n- No re-ownership bonus. K=0 in the new mode gives neutral EP selection; turning off the function preserves the old strategies.\n- API checks owner; saving the new mode does not erase the remaining filters. Worker uses fresh context, the outdated answer does not replace the current squad.\n- Deadline crossing, rating update, 429/503, unknown tournament length, stale ownership and incomplete player mapping have distinct causes, without made-up meanings.\n\nPerformance: compare the current and new implementation on the same fixed Sports.ru/FPL pools; minimum 20 measurements after warming up. p95 of a complete new selection ≤2.5× basic, the UI remains responsive due to the Worker. The cache fits within the listed limits, there is no increase in the number of requests for each player and there is no endless background polling. If the limit is exceeded, optimize the search instead of silently turning off the EP loss check.\n\n## 10. Utility testing and release\n\nFunctional tests prove compliance with restrictions, not improvement in athletic performance. The available data currently does not provide a complete, honest backtest of the global rank: the history of the images is short, the history of the entire field is not collected.\n\nBefore product inclusion, check the quality of the EP, the correctness of the sources and the actual loss relative to the database using the available images before the deadline. Candidate α/c/η/weights/εmax should be selected separately from FPL and Sports.ru separated by time, not from future results of the same round. If there is not enough data for the provider, the profile is marked uncalibrated and is available as an experimental opt-in.\n\nImplementation procedure:\n\n1. Sources, FPL ownership fix, calendar policy, checking ER on live completed rounds.\n2. Prisma-context, freshness, bounded cache and pure math with tests.\n3. Unified assessment and search with B, worker, integration tests.\n4. UI/API/saving, explanation of the actual price of the strategy.\n5. Local check, performance profile, duplicate/memory check; Existing mandatory repository checks on affected layers.\n6. Experimental opt-in separately per provider/tournament. Expand inclusion after accumulating data, do not claim proven rank gains without relevant history.\n\nFirst version completion criterion: user with associated command and valid data receives reproducible global K and valid squad with explicit EP comparison; If there are problems with the source, it continues to use the usual selection. There is no work with mini-leagues or manual entry of rating numbers.\n",

      "text": "---\nstatus: active\n---\n\n<a name=\"root\"></a>\n\n# FEAT-002: Global strategy formula {#root}\n\n<a name=\"plain-language\"></a>\n\n## Plain language {#plain-language}\n\nA separate strategyScore adjusts squad selection away from the popular template using rank and the gap to the leader. Player expected points must not be replaced with popularity. The first version is a bounded heuristic, rather than an estimate of the probability of winning.\n\nThe full text dated 2026-09-06 is preserved below and in `docs/archive/planning/GLOBAL_STRATEGY_FORMULA_RECOMMENDATIONS.md`.\n\n<a name=\"goal\"></a>\n\n## 1. Goal {#goal}\n\nFix the formula K and strategyScore, which is implemented by FEAT-001, along with starting coefficients and a ban on replacing EP with popularity.\n\n<a name=\"governing-specs\"></a>\n\n## 2. Governing specifications {#governing-specs}\n\n- Related: `spec://modules/machete/FEAT-001-global-ranking-strategy#root`\n\n<a name=\"scope\"></a>\n\n## 3. Scope {#scope}\n\n<a name=\"scope.in\"></a>\n\n### 3.1. In scope {#scope.in}\n\nFormulas q, g, u, K; separate strategyScore; EP loss budget; versioned profiles FPL and Sports.ru.\n\n<a name=\"scope.out\"></a>\n\n### 3.2. Out of scope {#scope.out}\n\nSelection of overtaking probability, mini-leagues, replacing EP with AdjustedEP.\n\n<a name=\"actors\"></a>\n\n## 4. Participants and triggers {#actors}\n\nSame squad-planner user as FEAT-001. The formula does not have a separate screen.\n\n<a name=\"scenarios\"></a>\n\n## 5. Scenarios {#scenarios}\n\nK is growing with a worse position, a larger lag and a smaller share of the remaining season. The leader receives K=0. Lack of ownership disables the strategic premium rather than being treated as 0%.\n\n<a name=\"data\"></a>\n\n## 6. Data and state {#data}\n\nN, rank, G, T, R, ER, ownership of the same provider. Parameters α/c/η/weights/εmax are starting engineering hypotheses, an unselected optimum.\n\n<a name=\"contracts\"></a>\n\n## 7. Contracts {#contracts}\n\nFormulas and examples from the source document. Core - pure functions FEAT-001; this document does the math.\n\n<a name=\"errors\"></a>\n\n## 8. Errors and validation {#errors}\n\nIncorrect N/rank/G/ER are not corrected by an arbitrary clamp. Lack of ownership blocks the recruitment allowance for candidates.\n\n<a name=\"traceability\"></a>\n\n## 9. Implementation traceability {#traceability}\n\nPure functions and profiles: `src/machete/global-strategy.ts`, `src/machete/global-strategy-config.ts`. Direct formula tests: `src/machete/global-strategy.test.ts`. Applying the budget to the roster and transfers: `src/machete/global-strategy-planner.ts` and the test of the same name.\n\n<a name=\"acceptance\"></a>\n\n## 10. Acceptance criteria {#acceptance}\n\nFive numerical examples of the original document are reproduced to tolerance 1e-6. The football player's EP does not mutate.\n\n<a name=\"relationships\"></a>\n\n## 11. Related specifications {#relationships}\n\n- Related: `spec://modules/machete/FEAT-001-global-ranking-strategy#root`\n\n<a name=\"changelog\"></a>\n\n## 12. Changelog {#changelog}\n\n- 2026-09-28: English documentation, repaired document references, and GitHub navigation anchors (WI-039).\n\n- [2026-09-07] Canon activated on the direct instructions of the user to implement the strategy.\n\n- [2026-09-06] Imported the current canon from `docs/archive/planning/GLOBAL_STRATEGY_FORMULA_RECOMMENDATIONS.md`.\n\n## Source document\n\nHistorical text as of import date; The execution status is checked against WI-001.\n\n# Adjustments to the global strategy formula\n\nDate: 2026-09-06. Status: proposal for implementation and testing, not implemented.\n\n## 1. Purpose\n\nThe function automatically selects the degree of deviation from popular players based on the global position of the linked team. Sports.ru - a separate field for each tournament and season; FPL — Overall of the current season. Mini-leagues and manual rating entry are not included in the task.\n\nThe proposed first version is a limited squad selection heuristic. It does not estimate the probability of winning, does not guarantee an improvement in final placing, and does not convert possessions into additional expected points. To optimize the probability of overtaking, distributions of results and joint outcomes of the squad and opponents are needed.\n\n## 2. What to change and why\n\n| Was in offer | Offered | Reason |\n|---|---|---|\n| AdjustedEP replaces EP | Save EP; enter a separate strategyScore | Popularity does not change the expected result of a football player |\n| Ownership from undefined field | Only global ownership of the same provider, tournament and season | Sports.ru EPL and FPL have different management teams and rules |\n| ln(N)/ln(500) multiplies the entire K | Remove separate FieldFactor | With millions of participants it inflates K; N is already participating in the percentile |\n| RankRisk = q^1.3 | Start with linear q | Power 1.3 reduces all internal values with respect to q; there is no empirical basis for it |\n| GapRisk = G/(G + ER × R) and separate urgency | Normalize G in normal round units; time to be taken into account separately | We remove the repeated amplification of time and the false interpretation of ER × R as an available wagering |\n| Urgency = 10/(R+10) is added even to the leader | Urgency multiplies the need for the chase and depends on the share of the season completed | The leader does not become more aggressive just because the end is approaching; seasons of different lengths are comparable |\n| Kfinal = 0.05 + 0.90K | Explicit clamp and permissible K=0 | Linear transformation does not guarantee boundaries and creates mandatory aggression |\n| Large rarity bonus with no loss limit | Small bonus plus strict EP loss budget of the entire solution | Rare weak player should not win due to one multiplier |\n| One setting for all | Versible profiles FPL and SPORTS_RU, Sports.ru has overrides for the tournament | Points, calendar, transfers, bonuses and field scale are different |\n| Unknown ownership is treated as 0% | Use a fresh source; on failure, disable strategic surcharge for request | Lack of data does not mean rarity |\n\n## 3. Proposed formula of the first version\n\nAll parameters below are starting engineering hypotheses. They check the implementation and constrain its behavior; These are not statistically selected optimal odds.\n\nDesignations:\n\n- N — global field size; rank — the team’s position according to the same rating section.\n- G = leaderPoints − managerPoints, non-negative lag. A negative difference means inconsistent data, not the need to silently reset G.\n- T - the total number of comparable rounds of this classification; R is the number of remaining rounds that can still be influenced before the deadline. R is not equal to the forecast horizon H.\n- ER - positive scale of points for one round, automatically assessed based on official completed rounds. This is a unit of normalization, not an estimate of available wagering.\n\n```text\nq = (rank − 1) / (N − 1)\ng = G / (G + c × ER)\nu = clamp((T − R) / max(T − 1, 1), 0, 1)^η\n\nK = clamp(Kmax × u × (wRank × q + wGap × g), 0, Kmax)\n```\n\nStarting profile: `c=3`, `η=2`, `wRank=0.4`, `wGap=0.6`, `Kmax=0.8`. The weights are summed up in 1. Separate configurations are created for FPL and Sports.ru; before calibration, the same values ​​are acceptable. There is no need to invent differences in coefficients without data.\n\nIf the team is in the lead or shares the lead on points - K=0. When R=0 - the season/available solutions are over, there are no recommendations. Until the first completed round - neutral mode. When N≤1, a competitive strategy is not needed. An inconsistent calendar or rating blocks automatic K.\n\nWhy this form: worsening place or increasing lag, all other things being equal, does not reduce K; decreasing R does not reduce it. The lag and place give a meaningful need for the pursuit, and u gradually increases its influence. The u square delays significant aggression until later in the season. This is a consciously conservative setting that needs to be checked, especially for short tournaments.\n\nK=0 for the leader means neutral selection according to the forecast. This is not a complete leadership defense strategy: global ownership does not describe the squad of second place. Protection cannot be promised in the interface.\n\n### Examples K\n\nWhen N=500, T=38, ER=70:\n\n| Location | G | R | K |\n|---|---:|---:|---:|\n| 1 | 0 | 1 | 0 |\n| 250 | 100 | 35 | 0.0021 |\n| 250 | 100 | 19 | 0.0829 |\n| 250 | 100 | 3 | 0.2814 |\n| 490 | 300 | 1 | 0.5959 |\n\nExamples check the arithmetic and direction of change of K; they do not prove athletic performance.\n\n## 4. Ownership and cost of deviation\n\nFor one “player-round” pair:\n\n```text\no = ownershipPercent / 100\nbonus = α × K × max(EP, 0) × (1 − o)\nstrategyScore = EP + bonus\n```\n\nStarting `α=0.15`: with Kmax=0.8 the premium does not exceed 12% positive EP. The negative prognosis remains; it does not improve by multiplying rarity. There is no need to repeatedly multiply the bonus by forecastConfidence or minutes in the first version: this can repeat the amendments already taken into account in the EP, and confidence is not the probability of a successful performance.\n\nEven a limited bonus is not enough. First, the basic feasible solution with the highest EP among the considered solutions is found, then the strategic one. For the same horizon, forecast source, set of restrictions and rules:\n\n```text\nε(K) = 0.03 × K / Kmax\nB = ε(K) × max(baseExpectedPoints, 0)\ncandidateExpectedPoints ≥ baseExpectedPoints − B\n```\n\n`baseExpectedPoints` and `candidateExpectedPoints` already take into account the real starting lineup, captain and transfer costs. If the base forecast is 70 and K=0.4, the allowed loss is 1.05 points. This is an upper limit, not a required sacrifice.\n\nBonus determines strategic sorting, and B separately limits its damage. In the UI, show both EP and loss relative to the base solution. Don't call strategyScore a score prediction.\n\nExample of the original problem: when K=0.8, the original formula prefers EP=6/O=5% (10.56) to the player EP=8/O=80% (9.28). The proposed premium gives 6.684 and 8.192 respectively. However, you need to check the budget of the entire squad, and not just an individual player.\n\n## 5. Fundamental restrictions\n\nThe expected separation of the squad from the fixed field in the simplified model is equal to `Σ(x_i − o_i)EP_i`, where x_i is its own exposure. The second part does not depend on our choice. Therefore, the positive contribution of the selected rare player cannot be presented as proof of optimality: missing players and joint outcomes must be taken into account.\n\nLow ownership does not prove high dispersion. The current upside mode in the project uses the spread of forecasts between rounds; This is also a calendar effect, and not a distribution of a random result. Do not use this spread as σ for the probability of winning and do not automatically add it to the new bonus.\n\nFor a captain, normal ownership does not equal effective field exposure: it does not include captaincy shares, starts or bonuses. In the first version, the bonus for normal possession is only a clearly indicated approximation. An effective holding that could exceed 100% would require a separate field and a different formula; it cannot be limited like a regular percentage.\n\nFalling short of first place in a multi-million dollar FPL is an aggressive goal. This version retains the original focus on the leader, but does not claim that such a target is better for the average final place. If a product needs optimization to reach a certain global top, this is a separate goal determination and verification, without mini-leagues.\n\n## 6. Data and sources\n\nVerified by 2026-09-06: public Sports.ru GraphQL returns `seasonScoreInfo.place/score/totalPlaces`, `totalSquadsCount`, rating `rating.squads` by SEASON without leagueID. In the Russian tournament at the time of inspection there were 18 926 teams, the leader was 478 points. `averageScore` in the verified answer was equal to 0, so the presence of the field cannot be taken as a usable statistic.\n\nFPL returns `total_players`, `summary_overall_rank`, `summary_overall_points`, `selected_by_percent`, events and Overall rating. In the current code, selectedByPercent is lost when upsert the main price table, although it is present in the saved data of all 653 players.\n\nPrimary research sources support modeling of rivals, variance, and correlations, but not the coefficients proposed here:\n\n- Haugh, Singal, How to Play Fantasy Sports Strategically (and Win): https://pubsonline.informs.org/doi/abs/10.1287/mnsc.2019.3528\n- Picking Winners in Daily Fantasy Sports Using Integer Programming: https://arxiv.org/abs/1604.01455\n- Verified FPL rating: https://fantasy.premierleague.com/api/leagues-classic/314/standings/\n- Sports.ru GraphQL: https://www.sports.ru/gql/graphql/\n\nDiscussions were used to find practical questions, not to prove coefficients: https://www.reddit.com/r/FantasyPL/comments/1f3942y/ ; https://stats.stackexchange.com/questions/212719/suggestions-for-simple-model-of-who-wins-the-league-in-a-game-of-fantasy-footbal\n\nDetailed specification: [GLOBAL_STRATEGY_SPEC.md](../../../docs/archive/planning/GLOBAL_STRATEGY_SPEC.md).\n",

      "lifecycle": "active",
      "summary": "",
      "text": "---\nstatus: active\n---\n\n<a name=\"root\"></a>\n\n# FEAT-004: RR - risk of rotation {#root}\n\n<a name=\"plain-language\"></a>\n\n## Plain language {#plain-language}\n\nEach player receives a rotation-risk indicator based on observed starts, bench appearances, and rest. This is an initial heuristic, rather than a calibrated probability.\n\n<a name=\"scope\"></a>\n\n## Scope {#scope}\nShow RR in the scheduler and apply in “Reliable”. EP forecast, balanced/upside/GLOBAL_AUTO and K modes do not change. Load and trend are not added separately; selection of weights and risk of injury outside the scope.\n\n<a name=\"goal\"></a>\n\n## Goal {#goal}\nMake rotation risk a separate explainable indicator using a user-approved formula, with the source data clearly available.\n\n<a name=\"actors\"></a>\n\n## Participants and triggers {#actors}\nThe squad-planner user sees RR and selects the selection mode. The existing ingestion populates the normalized history, and the background materializer updates the pool without asking for history on each render.\n\n<a name=\"governing-specs\"></a>\n\n## Governing specifications {#governing-specs}\nPlayer's own risk contract; Constraint: `spec://modules/machete/FEAT-001-global-ranking-strategy#contracts`.\n\n<a name=\"data\"></a>\n\n## Data and state {#data}\nSource - MatchPlayerStat.started/minutes and CoreMatch.finished/cancelled/matchDate by internal playerId. The schedule for the next match is taken from the same pool/tournament. Adds a compact rotationRisk field to the player DTO and RR version/expiration metadata to the existing snapshot JSON. There are no new tables or migrations.\n\n<a name=\"formula\"></a>\n\n## Formula {#formula}\nFor N = 50,20,10,5, take the last no more than N completed observations with known started. RN = BenchN / (StartsN + BenchN). started=false means known bench, including substitution; started=null and no entry do not mean benched. Duplicate matchIds are counted once. Incomplete windows use the actual number of observations with a small sample mark, without inventing missing games.\n\nrestDays — full intervals of 24 hours between the last completed match of a player with minutes>0 and the nearest future kickoff. Scale 0–1/2/3/4/5+ days: 1/0.72/0.40/0.16/0 (the original 0.25/0.18/0.10/0.04/0 is divided by 0.25).\n\nRR = clamp(0.15 R50 + 0.20 R20 + 0.25 R10 + 0.25 R5 + 0.15 Rrest, 0, 1).\n\nWith zero history, unknown rest or kickoff, the result is null with a reason. No data from the future: completed matches must be before min(observedAt, kickoff). The result contains version RR_V1, kickoff, observedAt, four compact windows and reasons; the full history is not transmitted to the client.\n\n<a name=\"contracts\"></a>\n\n## Contracts {#contracts}\nClean core `src/machete/rotation-risk.ts`; loading `src/server/rotation-risk.ts`. For one pool, IDs are deduplicated; one SQL query per batch for up to 250 players, up to 50 history lines per player and last appearance, no HTTP requests and permanent additional cache. The history is limited to existing FotMob normalized observations across all uploaded competitions; the indicator does not indicate the completeness of missing squads.\n\nPool/DTO/Worker is passing `rotationRisk`. In “Reliable” with a suitable RR score = max(0, consensusEP × (1−RR) × (0.75+0.25 confidence) × sourceMultiplier). confidence has an existing fallback 0.55, sourceMultiplier is preserved. The previous minutes/appearance/number risk notes penalties are not re-added. If the RR is unknown or outdated, the same reliable score is retained. The RR for the nearest match is used as a current criterion for the reliability of the lineup, not as the probability of all matches on the horizon.\n\nRR has a TTL of 24 hours and expires no later than kickoff. Snapshots from RR_V1 are updated sequentially when there is no version or TTL has expired; the old pool remains readable. Changes in the initial history are reflected during the next pool update.\n\n<a name=\"scenarios\"></a>\n\n## Scenarios {#scenarios}\nThe “RR%” column is available by default in new table settings and in the selection of existing columns. Tooltip explains windows/rest/data shortage. An unknown/obsolete RR is shown as “—”, without being replaced by 0%. The RR is unknown until the light pool is enriched.\n\n<a name=\"errors\"></a>\n\n## Errors and validation {#errors}\nNO_KNOWN_LINEUPS, UNKNOWN_REST and NO_FUTURE_FIXTURE result in null. SHORT_HISTORY allows calculation on actual windows and is shown as a sampling constraint. An expired/foreign version or an incorrect RR numeric value is not used. DB errors remain errors from the existing pool update; The previous READY snapshot is saved by the standard mechanism.\n\n<a name=\"traceability\"></a>\n\n## Implementation traceability {#traceability}\n`src/machete/rotation-risk.ts`, `src/server/rotation-risk.ts`, `src/machete/squad_logic.ts`, `src/machete/squad_planner.ts`, `src/machete/fantasy-player-pool-snapshots.ts`, `src/components/machete/FantasySquadPlanner.tsx`.\n\n<a name=\"acceptance\"></a>\n\n## Acceptance criteria {#acceptance}\nExample R50=.2,R20=.25,R10=.3,R5=.4,rest=2 gives .363. All rest thresholds, 0/1 boundaries, empty/short history, duplicates, unknown statuses and future data are covered by tests. DTO, reliable fallback/EP saving, batching and column preferences have been tested. Production build and change-related checks pass. Live DB smoke runs when the environment is available; is not replaced by a statement about the completeness of the data.\n\n<a name=\"relationships\"></a>\n\n## Related specifications {#relationships}\nRelated: `spec://modules/machete/FEAT-003-squad-player-card#root`.\n\n<a name=\"changelog\"></a>\n\n## Changelog {#changelog}\n\n- 2026-09-28: English documentation, repaired document references, and GitHub navigation anchors (WI-039).\n- [2026-09-07] User instructed to add RR; accepted baseline with normalized rest, without load/trend.\n",

      "text": "---\nstatus: active\n---\n\n# FEAT-006: Популярные игроки Sports перед туром {#root}\n\n## Простыми словами {#plain-language}\n\nСобираем опубликованные Sports списки покупок, продаж, популярных игроков и капитанов. Цель — до 15 игроков в каждой доступной категории; 10 или меньше допустимы. Если данных нет, честно показываем отсутствие данных. Процент владения игроком не выдаём за число покупок.\n\n## Цель {#goal}\n\nСделать опубликованные рейтинги доступными в контексте выбранного тура с проверяемым источником и временем. Источники и фактическая проверка: [исследование](../../work/evidence/WI-030/research.md#sports).\n\n## Управляющие документы {#governing-specs}\n\nLegacy product canon: `specs/common/main.md`. Это проект новой возможности, реализация отдельно. Потребитель: `spec://modules/telegram/FEAT-007-deadline-assistant#message`.\n\n## Участники и запуск {#actors}\n\nОбщий worker собирает публичные источники по расписанию и по запросу pipeline; пользователь читает готовый рейтинг выбранного тура на сайте или в боте. Оператор разбирает ошибку mapping/источника без ручного заполнения выдуманных значений.\n\n## Границы {#scope}\n\nВходит: публичный HTTP/GraphQL Sports, нормализация, привязка к сезону/турниру/туру, общий снимок трендов, чтение сайтом и ботом. Не входит: обход закрытого состава, сбор всех команд пользователей для вычисления рынка, замена FantasyPlayerPrice, автоматические покупки игроков или пересчёт моделей прогнозов.\n\n## Источники и семантика {#sources}\n\n1. Лента `https://www.sports.ru/fantasy-sports/news/` и связанные статьи Sports с явным турниром, сезоном и туром. Разбор основного HTML статьи, без комментариев, рекомендаций и рекламы. JSON-LD используется для canonical URL и даты; `articleBody` может содержать только анонс — это подтверждено проверкой.\n2. Уже используемый `https://www.sports.ru/gql/graphql/`, `fantasyQueries.players.list.status.selectedBy`: доля выбора, в проекте `FantasyPlayerPrice.selectedByPercent`. Это источник категории `OWNERSHIP`, а не подтверждённых покупок. Не добавлять запрос на каждого подписчика.\n3. История сравнимых снимков ownership может дать `OWNERSHIP_DELTA_PP`. Рост 20% → 23% означает +3 процентных пункта, а не 3 покупки и не доказанный чистый трансферный поток. Состав участников мог измениться.\n4. Официальные популярные трансферы тура: `fantasyQueries.squadTourInfo(input: { squadID, tourID }).topTransferPlayers` возвращает глобальный для сезона и тура список Sports (одинаков для любой команды лиги), порядок задаёт Sports; значение виджета — очки игрока, точное число трансферов API не отдаёт. Расширенный список сверх официального топа строится по `players.status.chartSelectedBy` как изменение доли выбора между выбранным туром и предыдущим; это неофициальный рейтинг и он так помечается. Запрос выполняется один раз на турнир/тур bounded-сборщиком, не на пользователя.\n\nКатегории: `BUYS`, `SELLS`, `OWNERSHIP`, `CAPTAINS`, `OWNERSHIP_DELTA_PP`. Статья может называть список покупками и приводить изменение популярности: сохранять исходную категорию и отдельно `metricKind=reported_popularity_change`, единицу и формулировку источника; не превращать проценты в количество. Фильтр «топ-1000 менеджеров» хранить как `populationScope`, не смешивать со всеми менеджерами. Поздняя публикация не гарантированно существует к 09:00.\n\n## Сценарии {#scenarios}\n\n- Один общий collector проверяет ленту каждые 30 минут, только для поддерживаемых текущих соревнований; дедлайн-процесс также запрашивает обновление в 08:10 МСК. Совпавшие запуски объединяются одним lease.\n- Новая релевантная статья разбирается один раз; изменившаяся статья получает новую ревизию по hash содержимого. Сбор прекращается после дедлайна для его утреннего отчёта; поздние публикации могут сохраняться как история.\n- Из каждого опубликованного рейтинга берём первые `min(15, available)` уникальных игроков в исходном порядке. Списки по позициям не называются общим топом. Для OWNERSHIP из API общий топ-15 строится только после полной пагинации всего пула, а не из 15 самых дорогих игроков.\n- Сайт/бот показывают категорию, значение и единицу, количество найденных игроков, время публикации/среза, ссылку Sports и ограничения выборки. Предлагаемое место на сайте — блок «Популярное на Sports» в контексте выбранного тура Squad; существующие таблицы и действия сохраняются.\n- Нет статьи нужного тура → `NOT_PUBLISHED`. Можно отдельно показать свежий OWNERSHIP с корректной подписью. Статья прошлого тура не подставляется молча.\n\n## Данные и идентичность {#data}\n\nПредлагаемые новые таблицы, без копирования каталога игроков:\n\n| Сущность | Основные поля и ограничения |\n|---|---|\n| `SportsTrendSource` | canonicalUrl UNIQUE, sourcePublishedAt, fetchedAt, contentHash, parserVersion, parseStatus; URL только разрешённого Sports host |\n| `SportsTrendSnapshot` | contestId, season, providerRoundId, category, metricKind, unit, populationScope, sourceId, revision, status, observedAt; UNIQUE(sourceId, revision, category, populationScope) |\n| `SportsTrendEntry` | snapshotId, sourceRank, providerPlayerId nullable, playerId nullable, sourceName, sourceTeam, sourcePosition, value nullable; UNIQUE(snapshotId, sourceRank) |\n\nРанжирование, dedup и mapping выполняются до публикации снимка. Существующие provider IDs/маппинг приоритетны. Для статьи без ID допустимо строгое уникальное совпадение имени, клуба/сборной и позиции в данном сезоне; неоднозначность остаётся `UNMAPPED`, имя источника сохраняется. Повтор одного игрока внутри одной категории отклоняется либо объединяется с evidence; не удалять одноимённого другого игрока. Национальные команды и клубы не взаимозаменяемы.\n\nТрансферные снимки используют `sourceId=null` и `sectionKey` `TRANSFERS_OFFICIAL` (официальный топ-3 Sports, `metricKind=reported_points`, `unit=points`), `TRANSFERS_GAIN`/`TRANSFERS_DROP` (расширенный топ-10 роста/падения доли, `metricKind=ownership_delta_pp`, `unit=pp`) и явный `statusReason` о том, что это доля выбора, а не точное число трансферов. Без предыдущего тура в графике запись не создаётся.\n\nТур определяется по метаданным и тексту статьи, не только по дате публикации. Если связь с туром недоказуема, запись не используется как тренд этого дедлайна. Снимки без round допустимы только для явно помеченного текущего ownership.\n\n## Контракты {#contracts}\n\nПредлагаемый read API: `GET /api/machete/sports-trends?contestId=...&providerRoundId=...`. Использует существующую session auth, возвращает `status`, категории, источник, timestamps, `availableCount`, до 15 entries каждой категории. Нет данных — нормальный ответ `NOT_PUBLISHED`, а ошибка парсера отдельно `FAILED`. Персональных данных в общем снимке нет. Бот читает тот же материализованный результат.\n\nParser/collector не вызывает AI на каждый материал или пользователя. Ограничения начального запуска: concurrency=1, до 3 страниц ленты и 20 новых/изменённых статей за цикл, таймаут HTTP 20 секунд, тело до 2 MiB, максимум 3 попытки с backoff/jitter. `429` учитывает Retry-After; защитная страница/403 не обходится и не распознаётся как пустой рейтинг.\n\n## Ошибки, кэш и хранение {#errors}\n\nНевалидные единицы, конфликт турнира/тура, повторные ranks, невозможные проценты или неполная загрузка запрещают публикацию новой версии. Сохраняется прежняя версия того же тура с явной отметкой времени/ошибки. Отсутствующее значение остаётся `null`, а не 0. В существующем `sportsRuSelectedByPercent` есть `Number(value)`: новый контракт требует проверить `null` до преобразования, иначе `Number(null)` создаст ложный ноль.\n\nHTTP conditional requests при поддержке сервера; кэш ленты 30 минут. Хранить нормализованные рейтинги 90 дней, ограниченное parser evidence максимум 7 дней и 100 MiB суммарно. Полные статьи постоянно не архивировать. История ownership: один общий снимок на contest/временной bucket, максимум 30 дней; удаление батчами. Логи не содержат пользовательских профилей и HTML.\n\n## Трассировка {#traceability}\n\nБудущие точки `@spec`: каталог `src/providers/sports-ru-trends/`, новый trends service в `src/machete/`, collector, read API и прямые parser/contract tests. Существующий ценовой импорт остаётся владельцем текущего selectedBy; новый модуль владеет рейтингами и историей, не дублирует импорт цен. Эти новые компоненты пока не реализованы.\n\n## Критерии готовности {#acceptance}\n\n- Реальный HTML пример распознаёт 10 покупок и 10 продаж; тест JSON-LD с коротким анонсом не теряет HTML-списки.\n- Проверены списки из 0/3/10/15/более 15 игроков, позиции, капитаны, выборка топ-1000, запятые в процентах, повторная публикация и неоднозначное имя.\n- OWNERSHIP, покупки и изменение ownership различаются в данных и UI; `null` не становится 0.\n- Точный тур/сезон, полная API-пагинация, source URL и freshness проверяются до показа; поздняя публикация не считается доступной к 09:00 задним числом.\n- Официальный топ трансферов совпадает с `topTransferPlayers` Sports; расширенный топ строится по дельте доли выбора, помечается как неофициальный и не подменяет точное число трансферов.\n- Повтор цикла не создаёт дубли; есть bounded retention, размер кэша и метрики skipped/failed/unmapped.\n\n## Связи и REVIEW {#relationships}\n\n- `spec://modules/telegram/INFRA-005-deadline-pipeline#pipeline` — координация обновления.\n- REVIEW: поддержка конкретных чемпионатов подтверждается fixture corpus и наблюдением публикаций; наличие одной статьи не доказывает регулярную публикацию во всех лигах. Первый выпуск собирает только соревнования с уже синхронизированным расписанием; остальные лиги не включаются до наблюдения.\n- UI-размещение определено: панель «Популярное на Sports» в правой колонке Squad для выбранного тура.\n\n## История изменений {#changelog}\n\n- 2026-09-25: создан draft на основании HTTP-проверки с production и исследования текущего импорта.\n- 2026-09-25: переведён в `active` для реализации WI-031. Согласовано с человеком: сборщик включается флагом `SPORTS_TRENDS_SYNC_ENABLED` (по умолчанию off), ownership-снимки строятся из уже полностью пагинированного `FantasyPlayerPrice.selectedByPercent`, панель выводится для выбранного тура, статьи без доказанного тура не используются как тренд дедлайна.\n- 2026-09-25: WI-037 добавляет официальные популярные трансферы Sports (`topTransferPlayers`, глобальные на тур) и расширенный топ-10 роста/падения доли выбора по `chartSelectedBy`; точное число трансферов API не публикует.\n",
